# Agents · I · part 1

906 entries, i-ab to implementation-agent. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **i-ab**: Audit a merge commit for silently-discarded changes — the GSDL-715/720 failure mode where conflict resolution keeps stale branch content and reverts already-landed fixes. Use after git merge origin/main to triage raw flags into confirmed regressions, false positives, and self-healed findings before anything reaches git history. Standalone audit tool; /i-crpr orchestrates this automatically as part of PR creation. ([idallasjlabs/agentshroud](https://github.com/idallasjlabs/agentshroud/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/i-ab.agent.md) · 5 copies)
- **i-agile**: Apply Agile principles to iterative planning, backlog refinement, sprint ceremonies, and velocity tracking. Use when structuring delivery for a feature team, running sprint planning, or coaching on Agile practices. Produces sprint plans, refined backlogs, and delivery forecasts. ([idallasjlabs/agentshroud](https://github.com/idallasjlabs/agentshroud/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/i-agile.md) · 5 copies)

Produces an ADR with tradeoffs and risks. ([idallasjlabs/agentshroud](https://github.com/idallasjlabs/agentshroud/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/i-architecture-review.md) · 5 copies)

- **i-code-reviewer**: Pre-commit peer review plus mandatory system-wide change impact analysis: lint and complexity, then changed files mapped to jobs, tables, queries, consumers and schedules. - **i-cr**: DEPRECATED — use /i-code-reviewer. Conduct security-focused code review with production safety checklist: blast radius, rollback path, test isolation, Athena cost guards (LIMIT/partition WHERE), and OWASP Top 10. Use on every PR as the reviewer — not the author. The 400-line rule, IAM checks, and data safety audit are mandatory outputs. ([idallasjlabs/agentshroud](https://github.com/idallasjlabs/agentshroud/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/i-cr.agent.md) · 5 copies)
- **i-crpr**: Create a production-ready PR with mandatory merge regression pre-flight. Runs git merge --no-commit origin/main, audits the staged result with /i-ab --pending, offers to restore confirmed losses in-place before committing, then pushes and opens the PR with the audit summary embedded. Use instead of bare git merge + gh pr create — the primary guard against the GSDL-715/720 silent regression pattern. ([idallasjlabs/agentshroud](https://github.com/idallasjlabs/agentshroud/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/i-crpr.md) · 5 copies)
